ShinyHunters, an extortion group, is exploiting a known vulnerability in Oracle PeopleSoft systems, allowing them to bypass web application firewalls and execute commands on compromised servers. This has led to data theft incidents affecting various sectors, including education and government, with some claims of accessing sensitive FBI data.

 - ShinyHunters is using a URL-encoding trick to exploit Oracle PeopleSoft vulnerabilities more effectively.
 - The group has successfully executed commands and stolen data from a range of sectors, prompting urgent security recommendations.

This problematic update, KB5002907, was initially aimed at facilitating the update of out-of-date Microsoft 365 Apps but inadvertently affected perpetual licenses, prompting complaints from multiple users and administrators.

 - Users are experiencing deactivation and removal of Office 2016 and Office 2019 due to a recent update.
 - Microsoft has paused the update rollout while investigating the reported issues.

Category: Cybersecurity
Importance: 8
Two third-party GitHub Actions that were previously compromised in a malware campaign were re-enabled, allowing workflows to download malicious code for over a week before being disabled again. The malware, part of the Mini Shai-Hulud supply-chain attack, targets developers' tokens and credentials, posing a significant risk to those relying on these actions. Developers are advised to remove affected actions or pin verified clean commits to mitigate the risks.

 - GitHub Actions were re-enabled with malicious payloads still active after prior compromise.
 - The Mini Shai-Hulud attack targeted developers' tokens and credentials.
 - Developers are advised to remove or pin these actions to avoid exposure.

Source: https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/github-actions-re-enabled-with-mini-shai-hulud-payload-still-active/

The 8087's architecture, utilizing an 80-bit format for floating-point numbers, allowed for greater accuracy and included mechanisms for managing various arithmetic operations efficiently.

This crisis led to increased advocacy for animal rights and the establishment of the ASPCA, spearheaded by Henry Bergh, who pushed for stricter animal protection laws in response to the cruelty observed during the epidemic.

The commercial real estate sector is facing significant financial challenges as many office properties are experiencing extreme declines in value and increasing loan delinquencies. Defaults on office loans packaged into commercial mortgage-backed securities (CMBS) have reached distress levels surpassing those after the 2008 financial crisis, with substantial portions of major cities like Chicago and Denver seeing extremely high vacancy rates. As loan maturities approach and extensions become less common, actual losses are converting from theoretical to real, prompting a shift in property ownership at dramatically reduced valuations.

 - Office loan delinquencies are at an all-time high, surpassing levels seen post-2008 crisis.
 - Major cities are experiencing severe commercial real estate value declines, particularly those with older office buildings.
 - Investors are beginning to purchase distressed properties at reduced prices, marking a significant shift in the market.
